Elliptic curve parameters:
name = secp17k1
mode = test

n = 99667

Directly constructed signatures for taxonomy classes A B E. z is CHOSEN so that each signature lands in its class -- it is not the hash of any particular message. Every row is nonetheless an honest ECDSA signature: it verifies against x*G, and the private key x* is re-derived from the public (z, r, s) by the recovery routine and confirmed on the curve. Constructing such a signature requires already knowing x, so none of this transfers to a signature you did not create yourself.
